# Day 3 — Full Audio Transcript (September 17, 2026)

**Source:** complete Day 3 broadcast audio (7h 58m; 8:30 AM–4:28 PM Pacific), machine-transcribed with faster-whisper (large-v3, batched). Timestamps are broadcast-elapsed; wall Pacific = audio + 8:30 AM. The first spoken line lands at 00:09:56 (8:40 AM) after the opening hold cards, and the sign-off ends at 07:57:43 (4:27 PM).

**Accuracy:** machine transcription — product names garble ("Grockbot"/"GrockBot" = Grok Bot, "SpaceX AI" = xAI, "Gwakbap"/"RockBot" = Grok Bot, "ThursdayArenas.com" = thursdayarena.com, "Potato" = Lauren's on-stream nickname, "Blake Shuler" = Blake Schuller, "P stack" = P-Stack). No speaker labels; identify from context. Word errors are left as transcribed.

**Session map (audio time → content):**
- 00:09:56–00:28:23 | 8:40–8:58 AM | Trio open: intros, the ten-minute free-month promo for new Grok Bot users ($200 value), the overnight software-factory recap (100+ PRs landed overnight, 168 by the count; Potato Mode autopilot; the QA bot Play), a code-name-Cupcake game preview, and the handoff downstairs.

- 03:57:15–04:46:11 | 12:27–1:16 PM | Main stage: Blake [Schuller], "Grok Bot for Post Sales" — Gus and the bot-team demos, the self-improvement scan, Q&A.
